{"batchcomplete":"","query":{"pages":{"-1":{"ns":0,"title":"2020 in KSW","missing":""},"-2":{"ns":0,"title":"2021 in KSW","missing":""},"-3":{"ns":0,"title":"2022 in KSW","missing":""},"-4":{"ns":0,"title":"2023 in KSW","missing":""},"-5":{"ns":0,"title":"2024 in KSW","missing":""},"-6":{"ns":0,"title":"2025 in KSW","missing":""},"-7":{"ns":0,"title":"2026 in KSW","missing":""},"-8":{"ns":0,"title":"2027 in KSW","missing":""},"52428112":{"pageid":52428112,"ns":0,"title":"Absolute Championship Akhmat","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","*":"{{short description|MMA, kickboxing and BJJ promoter based in Russia}}\n{{Infobox sports league\n| title = Absolute Championship Akhmat\n| current_season = 2026 in Absolute Championship Akhmat\n| logo = Absolute_Championship_Akhmat_logo.png\n| logo_size = 200px \n| founder = Mairbek Khasiev\n| president = [[Magomed Bibulatov]]\n| headquarters = [[Grozny]], Russia\n| sport = [[Mixed martial arts]]\n| website = [https://www.aca-mma.com/en www.aca-mma.com/en]\n| country = Russia\n| founded = {{start date and age|df=yes|2014||}}\n}}\n'''Absolute Championship Akhmat''' ('''ACA'''), formerly known as '''Absolute Championship Berkut''' ('''ACB'''), is a Russian [[mixed martial arts]], [[kickboxing]], and [[Brazilian jiu-jitsu]] organization and one of the leading promotions in Europe.{{cite web|last=Zidan |first=Karim |url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2014/12/22/7435743/chechen-acb-president-bekhan-khasiev-confident-his-promotion-will-garner-recognition-mma-interview |title=Ripe with ambition, ACB President Bekhan Khasiev confident his promotion will garner international recognition |publisher=Bloody Elbow |date=2014-12-22 |access-date=2016-11-30}} To date, most events have been hosted in [[Grozny]], [[Chechnya]]. ACA has also held events in other Russian cities, as well as in [[Australia]], [[Austria]], [[Azerbaijan]], [[Belarus]], [[Belgium]], [[Brazil]], [[Canada]], [[China]], [[England]], [[France]], [[Georgia (country)|Georgia]], [[Germany]], [[Italy]], [[Kazakhstan]], the [[Netherlands]], [[Poland]], [[Romania]], [[Scotland]], [[Slovakia]], [[Tajikistan]], [[Turkey]], the [[United Arab Emirates]] and the [[United States]].\n\n==History==\n=== Absolute Championship Berkut ===\nACB was founded by Mairbek Khasiev, a resident of [[Chechnya]]. In 2009, he founded the Berkut Fight Club in Grozny which helped young fighters get the opportunity to compete at the highest level.\n\nSeveral fighters who were successful in ACB have gone on to sign with more well known promotions such as the UFC. ACB had its own fight team, the Fight Club Berkut, which consisted of some of the promotion's fighters, such as Beslan Isaev, [[Magomed Bibulatov]], Musa Khamanaev and Aslambek Saidov. ACB co-operated with other promotions in Europe by exchanging fighters, for example KSW and The Cage. ACB ran the majority of its shows live on a variety of TV networks (Match TV, Polsat Sport) throughout Europe. The promotion ran its shows live internationally on its Facebook page.\n\n=== International Expansion ===\nThe 2017 campaign featured 27 events spanning across thirteen countries, including the United States, Austria, Poland, England, Tajikistan, Belarus, Turkey, Kazakhstan, Canada, Brazil, Germany, Australia and Russia.\n\n=== TECH-Krep FC purchase ===\nOn September 12, 2018, Mairbek Khasiev revealed that Absolute Championship Berkut had purchased TECH-Krep FC.{{cite web|url=https://www.sports.ru/boxing/1067019311.html|title=ACB purchases TECH-Krep FC|publisher=sports.ru|date=September 12, 2018}} Khasiev went on to explain that TECH-Krep FC would cease operation and cancel their October 26 event, and that former Tech-Krep FC head Alexey Yatsenko would become the president of ACB.\n\n=== WFCA acquisition and integration ===\nOn November 28, 2018, the Head of the Chechen Republic [[Ramzan Kadyrov]] announced that the Absolute Championship Berkut (ACB) and the World Fighting Championship Akhmat (WFCA) would merge to form a single promotion.{{cite web | url=https://tass.ru/sport/5847959 | title=\u041a\u0430\u0434\u044b\u0440\u043e\u0432 \u0437\u0430\u044f\u0432\u0438\u043b \u043e\u0431 \u043e\u0431\u044a\u0435\u0434\u0438\u043d\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0438 \u043b\u0438\u0433 \u0441\u043c\u0435\u0448\u0430\u043d\u043d\u044b\u0445 \u0435\u0434\u0438\u043d\u043e\u0431\u043e\u0440\u0441\u0442\u0432 ACB \u0438 WFCA - \u0422\u0410\u0421\u0421 }}\n\n=== US Sanctions ===\n\nOn December 20, 2020, the [[U.S. Department of Treasury]] announced sanctions against ACA and Akhmat MMA due to their ownership by Kadyrov. Due to this, no American citizen or company can do business with them, meaning all American fighters could face fines or jail time if they fight for the organization.{{cite web |url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2020/12/16/22178613/us-sanctions-ofac-ramzan-kadyrov-chechnya-sportswashing-mma-politics-karim-zidan-feature|title=The Dictator's Dilemma: How U.S. sanctions will impact Ramzan Kadyrov's MMA aspirations|date=16 December 2020 |publisher=bloodyelbow.com}} [[Brett Cooper (fighter)|Brett Cooper]] was the first fighter to pull out of his fight at [[2020 in Absolute Championship Akhmat#ACA 116: Froes vs Balaev|ACA 116: Froes vs Balaev]] due to the sanctions.{{cite web age|df=yes|2016||}}\n}}\n\n'''BRAVE Combat Federation''' is a{{cite web|url=https://powcastsports.com/stephenlomanin-manila-for-brave/|title=FILIPINO STARS AT THE CORE OF BRAVE'S GLOBAL EXPANSION - Powcast Sports|date=29 January 2019}} [[mixed martial arts]] (MMA) [[promoter (entertainment)|promotion]] based in [[Bahrain]]. The organization was established on 23 September 2016 by Sheikh [[Khalid bin Hamad Al Khalifa]].{{cite web|url=http://gulf-insider.com/brave-new-world-interview/|title=A BRAVE New World - Interview - Gulf Insider - The Arabian Review|access-date=2017-02-23|archive-date=2020-10-01|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201001080021/https://www.gulf-insider.com/brave-new-world-interview/|url-status=dead}} BRAVE Combat Federation features mixed martial artists from more than 80{{Cite web |date=2022-10-23 |title=Burkina Faso becomes 80th country to be represented at BRAVE CF with Israel Mano|url=https://mmauk.net/2022/10/23/burkina-faso-becomes-80th-country-to-be-represented-at-brave-cf-with-israel-mano/ |access-date=2023-04-09 |website=mmauk.net}} nations located in five continents. BRAVE Combat Federation events are aired though multiple media partners including [[El Rey Network]], [[Globosat|Combate]], [[Myx TV]], [[ABS-CBN Sports and Action|S+A]] [[ESPN 5]] and [[Bahrain Radio and Television Corporation]].{{cite web|url=http://www.superlutas.com.br/noticias/57669/com-ex-ufc-no-card-BRAVE-3-anuncia-venda-de-ingressos-para-evento-no-brasil/|title=Com ex-UFC no card, BRAVE 3 anuncia venda de ingressos para evento no Brasil|date=22 February 2017}}{{cite web|url=https://esportes.yahoo.com/noticias/BRAVE-3-bananada-tira-sarro-110040480.html|title=BRAVE 3: Bananada tira sarro de grito caracter\u00edstico de ex-UFC e companheiros}} \n\nIn 2023, BRAVE Combat Federation set a new record for an MMA promotion hosting events in most number of nations after organizing events in 38 nations worldwide.{{cite web |last1=Head |first1=Simon |title=Brave CF set for Spain, Brazil, and Bahrain in busy finish to 2023 |url=https://www.fightersonlymag.com/latest-news/brave-cf-set-for-spain-brazil-and-bahrain-in-busy-finish-to-2023/ |website=The Fighters Only |date=9 October 2023 |publisher=The Fighters Only Magazine |access-date=18 October 2023}}{{Cite web |date=2026-06-05 |title=BRAVE CF confirms 10-bout lineup as European presence continues to grow {{!}} THE DAILY TRIBUNE {{!}} KINGDOM OF BAHRAIN |url=https://www.newsofbahrain.com/sports/134766.html |access-date=2026-06-23 |website=DT News |language=en-US}}\n\nThe organization was formally launched in 2016 and has established operations in [[Dublin]],{{cite web|url=http://fightstoremedia.ie/brave-combat-federation-coming-ireland/|title=BRAVE Combat Federation coming to Ireland - Fightstore Media|date=12 September 2016}} [[S\u00e3o Paulo]] and [[Mumbai]]{{cite web|url=http://sport360.com/article/other/202676/bahrains-brave-combat-federation-initiates-mma-expansion-to-india/|title=Bahrain's BRAVE Combat Federation initiates MMA expansion to India}} prior to launching events and for talent relations. BRAVE Combat Federation, alongside the holding company, KHK MMA and Bahrain Mixed Martial Arts Federation hosted the [[IMMAF]] World Championships in November, 2017,{{cite web|url=http://www.newsofbahrain.com/viewNews.php?ppId=30085&pid=21&MNU=|title=Bahrain to host IMMAF World Championships}} a partnership which was renewed for 2018 and 2019{{cite web |url=http://mma-mena.com/Newsdet.aspx?id=326|title=Bahrain to host 2018 and 2019 IMMAF World Championships|publisher=MMA Mena}}\n\nIn July 2021, BRAVE Combat Federation was nominated for the first time as Promotion of the Year on the World MMA Awards.{{cite web|url=https://www.worldmmaawards.com/nominees/|title=World MMA Awards Nominees|date=11 December 2019|publisher=Fighters Only}} The organization also has global strategic partnerships with fellow fight promoters [[Real Xtreme Fighting]] (RXF), Alash Pride FC{{Cite web |last=AgentMMA |date=2026-04-30 |title=Alash Pride FC and BRAVE CF announce joint event in Almaty on May 31 |url=https://agentmma.com/news/61791-alash-pride-fc-and-brave-cf-announce-joint-event-in-almaty-on-may-31 |access-date=2026-06-23 |website=Agent MMA |language=en}}, LFC, RXF, WEF, WFC, BFN, Krespost, PRO FC, RSM, Golden Cage, Oktagon, GEC, Beast CF, ATF, NFC, [[Hexagone MMA]], Fight2One, and Victorious Fighting and Entertainment.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/events/RXF-36-Brave-CF-28-VIP-Edition-81093|title=RXF 36 /Brave CF 28 VIP Edition|publisher=[[Sherdog]]|access-date=2020-11-02}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.tapology.com/fightcenter/events/70304-brave-cf|title=Brave CF 36|publisher=tapology.com|access-date=2020-11-02}}{{cite web |last1=Head |first1=Simon |title=Brave CF set for Spain, Brazil, and Bahrain in busy finish to 2023 |url=https://www.fightersonlymag.com/latest-news/brave-cf-set-for-spain-brazil-and-bahrain-in-busy-finish-to-2023/ |website=fightersonlymag |date=9 October 2023 |publisher=Fighters Only |access-date=26 October 2023}}\n\n==History==\n\n=== Overview ===\n[[File:Eldar Eldarov being pesented with the BRAVE CF Super Lightweight Championship, 2019.jpg|thumb|right|[[Eldar Eldarov]] at Brave CF 23 being presented with the [[Brave Combat Federation#Super Lightweight Championship|BRAVE CF Super Lightweight Championship]]]]\n\nKHK MMA is a [[Bahrain]] based mixed martial arts organization that promotes the growth of mixed martial arts in the region.{{Cite web|url=http://www.bna.bh/portal/en/news/737614|title=\u0639\u0646 \u0628\u0646\u0627 | \u0648\u0643\u0627\u0644\u0629 \u0623\u0646\u0628\u0627\u0621 \u0627\u0644\u0628\u062d\u0631\u064a\u0646}} KHK MMA Fight Team featured fighters from multiple promotions including [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]], [[Frankie Edgar]], [[Jose Torres (fighter)|Jose Torres]]{{Cite web|url=https://www.arabsmma.com/28680-2/|title=KHK MMA's Jose Torres Successful in his Professional Debut|author=Arabs MMA Staff|date=March 6, 2016|website=ArabsMMA}} and [[James Gallagher (fighter)|James Gallagher]].{{Cite web|url=http://www.bna.bh/portal/en/news/737140|title=\u0639\u0646 \u0628\u0646\u0627 | \u0648\u0643\u0627\u0644\u0629 \u0623\u0646\u0628\u0627\u0621 \u0627\u0644\u0628\u062d\u0631\u064a\u0646}} Coaches and UFC Hall of Famers like [[Renzo Gracie]], [[John Kavanagh (martial artist)|John Kavanagh]], Bob Schirmer, and [[Pete Williams (fighter)|Pete Williams]] were enlisted to work with KHK MMA.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2015/08/fighters-and-trainers-discuss-khk-chances-to-become-best-mma-camp-in-the-world|title=Fighters, trainers discuss KHK's chances to become world's best MMA camp|date=August 20, 2015}} \n\nBRAVE Combat Federation was established under KHK MMA. The promotion held its inaugural event, [[Brave 1: The Beginning]] took place in Isa Town, Bahrain, on September 23, 2016, a date which coincided with Sheikh [[Khalid bin Hamad Al Khalifa]]'s birthday.{{cite web|url=https://www.arabsmma.com/brave-1-fakhreddine-vs-vieira-fight-card/|title=BRAVE 1 \"The Beginning\" Fight Card + Results|date=23 September 2016|publisher=Arabs MMA}} During its initial phase, BRAVE CF expanded into markets such as Brazil{{cite web|url=https://www.arabsmma.com/brave-combat-federation-announces-brave-11-fight-card/|title=BRAVE Combat Federation announces BRAVE 11 fight card|date=27 March 2018|publisher=Arabs MMA}}, India,{{cite web|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/khamzat-chimaev-replaces-leon-aliu-takes-on-sidney-wheeler-at-brave-20/|title=Khamzat Chimaev replaces Leon Aliu, takes on Sidney Wheeler at BRAVE 20|date=15 December 2018|publisher=Fightbook MMA}} Indonesia,{{cite web|url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/mma/brave-12-khk-legacy-fight-card-announced|title=BRAVE 12: KHK Legacy fight card announced|date=28 April 2018 |publisher=Sports Keeda}} Mexico, Jordan{{cite web|url=http://www.mixedmartialarts.com/news/brave-announces-full-fight-card-for-jordan-debut|title=BRAVE announces full fight card for Jordan debut|date=22 January 2018 |publisher=Mixed Martial Arts}}, and the United Arab Emirates, positioning itself as a promotion focused on global talent development rather than a single-region model.{{cite web|url=https://www.arabsmma.com/brave-2-results-boudegzdame-submits-watson-style/|title=BRAVE 2 Results: Elias Boudegzdame Submits Watson With Style|date=2 December 2016|publisher=Arabs MMA}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.timeoutbahrain.com/sportandoutdoor/features/74651-mixed-martial-arts-in-bahrain|title=Mixed martial arts in Bahrain|website=Time Out Bahrain|date=30 November 2016 }} BRAVE CF also hosted the first-ever International Combat Week, in partnership with the [[IMMAF]].{{cite web|url=https://immaf.org/immaf-heads-attend-latest-immaf-world-championships-brave-9-press-conference-in-bahrain-2/|title=IMMAF heads attend latest IMMAF World Championships & BRAVE 9 press conference in Bahrain|date=25 October 2017 |publisher=IMMAF.org}}{{cite web|url=https://immaf.org/2018-2019-immaf-world-championships-announced-for-bahrain-2/|title=2018 & 2019 IMMAF WORLD CHAMPIONSHIPS ANNOUNCED FOR BAHRAIN|date=8 March 2018 |publisher=IMMAF.org}}\n\nBetween 2018 and 2021, BRAVE CF accelerated its international growth by hosting events across Europe, Asia, and Africa. The organization entered new markets including Northern Ireland{{cite web|url=http://mmauk.net/2018/05/22/full-fight-card-revealed-for-brave-13-europe-evolution-in-belfast/|title=Full Fight card revealed for BRAVE 13: Europe Evolution in Belfast|date=22 May 2018|publisher=MMA UK}}, Morocco, and Kazakhstan.{{cite web|url=https://www.arabsmma.com/brave-3-announced-in-brazil/|title=Mohammed Shahid Announces BRAVE 3 In Brazil|date=6 December 2016|publisher=Arabs MMA}}{{cite web |url=https://www.arabsmma.com/brave-3-brazil-success-results/|title=BRAVE 3 Brazil A Success. Check Out The Results.|date=20 March 2017|publisher=Arabs MMA}} The promotion emphasized partnerships with local organizations and regulatory bodies to develop MMA ecosystems in host countries. This period also saw an increase in athlete diversity, with fighters representing a wide range of nationalities. The events also showcased the former [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] champion and KHK MMA fighter [[Frankie Edgar]] as a color commentator{{cite web |url=https://www.bravefights.com/mma-legend-frankie-edgar-brave-3/)|title=MMA LEGEND FRANKIE EDGAR TO BE COLOR COMMENTATOR FOR BRAVE 3|publisher=Brave Fights}} Later, MMA legend and BRAVE ambassador [[Wanderlei Silva]].{{cite web|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/mma-icons-wanderlei-silva-and-frankie-edgar-witness-historical-brave-9-public-weigh-in/|title=MMA icons Wanderlei Silva and Frankie Edgar witness historical BRAVE 9 public weigh-in|publisher=Fightbook MMA}} BRAVE Combat Federation became the first global MMA promotion to host an event in [[Africa]],{{cite web|url=https://topsportsasia.com/2018/07/02/brave-cfs-brave-14-is-africas-first-international-mma-event/|title=BRAVE CF's 'BRAVE 14' is Africa's first international MMA event|publisher=Top Sports Asia}} with BRAVE 14, in [[Tangier]], [[Morocco]], on August 18.{{cite web|url=https://mmamotion.net/2018/07/24/report-fight-card-announced-for-brave-14-morocco/|title=Report: Fight card announced for BRAVE 14, Morocco|date=26 March 2019 |publisher=MMA Motion}} The event was hosted under the patronage of [[King Mohammed VI]], the king of [[Morocco]].{{cite web|url=http://www.gdnonline.com/Details/383110/Brave-14-to-be-hosted-under-Morocco-King-patronage|title=BRAVE 14 to be hosted under Morocco King patronage|date=5 December 2017 |publisher=Gulf Daily News}}\n\nBRAVE Combat Federation hosted its first show in [[Ljubljana]], Slovenia in 2020, in partnership with WFC.{{Cite news |title=Slovenia debut for Bahrain's BRAVE CF deemed a \"massive success\" |work=Bahrain News Agency |url=https://www.bna.bh/en/HRHPremierpatronisesInternationalDownstreamConference/HRHPremierpatronisesInternationalDownstreamConference/SloveniadebutforBahrainsBRAVECFdeemedamassivesuccess.aspx?cms=q8FmFJgiscL2fwIzON1%2BDklomjnQd1Yq8%2BQPlSpmres%3D}} The organization announced BRAVE CF 35 (Brazil), BRAVE CF 36 (Romania), and BRAVE CF 37 (Sweden). However, the shows were cancelled due to the coronavirus pandemic.{{Cite web |last=Clifton |first=Michael |date=2020-03-15 |title=BRAVE CF Indefinitely Postpones All Upcoming Events |url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/brave-cf-indefinitely-postpones-all-upcoming-events/ |access-date=2023-04-04}} BRAVE CF returned to action in July, with BRAVE CF 35 and BRAVE CF 36 taking place in [[Romania]],{{Cite web |last=|title=BRAVE Combat Federation announces dates for two shows in Romania |url=https://lockerroom.in/blog/view/BRAVE-CF-dates-for-Romania |access-date=2023-04-04 |website=lockerroom.in}} in closed venues.{{Cite web |title=FIGHTMAG|url=https://www.fightmag.com.au/ |access-date=2023-04-04 |website=FIGHTMAG}} BRAVE CF continued its expansion strategy by increasing the number of host countries and strengthening its presence in Europe and Asia.{{cite web|url=https://www.iol.co.za/sport/chipfumbu-to-hussein-hes-coming-into-the-jungle-at-brave-19-18369653|title=Chipfumbu to Hussein: He's coming into the jungle at BRAVE 19|publisher=IOL}} The organization has also focused on digital distribution and international broadcasting partnerships to expand its global audience reach.{{cite web|url=http://realfight.ph/mma/brave-stage-manila-card-2019/|title=BRAVE Combat Federation to stage Manila card in 2019|publisher=Real Fight}}{{cite web|url=https://www.thefightnation.com/brave-sets-15-march-for-philippines-debut/|title=BRAVE SETS 15 MARCH FOR PHILIPPINES DEBUT|publisher=The Fight Nation}}{{cite web|url=https://fightpost.co.uk/2019/07/29/vip-personalities-enchanted-by-brave-24-london-action/|title=VIP Personalities Enchanted by BRAVE 24: London Action|date=29 July 2019|publisher=Fight Post}}{{cite web|url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/brave-26-results-silva-earns-tko-victory-over-roa/|title=BRAVE 26 Results: Silva Earns TKO Victory Over Roa|date=8 September 2019 |publisher=Low Kick MMA}}\n\n== Roster ==\n{{Main|List of current Brave CF fighters}}\n\n==Rules==\nBRAVE Combat Federation follows the ''[[Unified Rules of Mixed Martial Arts]]'' that were originally established by the [[New Jersey State Athletic Control Board]] and modified by the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]].{{cite web |url=http://www.leg.state.nv.us/NAC/NAC-467.html |title=NSAC Regulations: CHAPTER 467 - UNARMED COMBAT |publisher=Nevada State Athletic Commission |access-date=February 8, 2012}}\n\nAfter the [[Association of Boxing Commissions]] approved the opening of new weight classes,{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/7/26/16037760/abc-approves-adoption-of-four-new-weight-classes-for-mma|title=ABC approves adoption of four new weight classes for MMA - MMA Fighting|date=26 July 2017 }} BRAVE announced it would become the first international promotion to introduce a Super Lightweight division, as well as pushing the Welterweight limit to 175 lbs.{{cite web|url=https://www.mixedmartialarts.com/editorial/brave-announces-new-weight-classes-for-2019:18396f5c-da8c-427c-97a2-4fc8aba8e8f1|title=BRAVE announces new weight classes for 2019 - Mixed Martial Arts}}\n\n==Weight divisions and champions==\n{{See also|Mixed martial arts weight classes}}\n\nBRAVE Combat Federation has following weight classes:\n{| class=\"wikitable\" web|url=http://www.gdnonline.com/Details/320154/MMA-expert-Jenness-joins-Brave-commentary-panel|title=MMA expert Jenness joins BRAVE commentary panel|date=5 December 2017 |publisher=Gulf Daily News}}\n\n=== Broadcast Talent ===\nThe BRAVE Combat Federation broadcast team consists of Phil Campbell, Kirik Jenness, and Carlos Kremer. Kremer has been the promotion\u2019s official cage announcer since BRAVE CF 1, making him one of the longest-serving on-air figures in the organization\u2019s history. In recognition of his contributions, he was inducted into the BRAVE CF Hall of Fame{{Cite web |date=2017-12-05 |title=Other Sports: Kremer named to BRAVE Hall of Fame |url=https://www.gdnonline.com/Details/1363980 |access-date=2026-06-23 |website=www.gdnonline.com}} during BRAVE CF 100, becoming the first cage announcer to receive this honor.\n\n==== Phil Campbell ====\nPhilip Laurence Campbell{{Cite web |title=Phil Campbell {{!}} MMA Fighter Page |url=https://www.tapology.com/fightcenter/fighters/63808-phil-campbell |access-date=2025-11-15 |website=Tapology |language=en}} (born 9 October 1988) is an Irish broadcaster and former combat sports athlete who serves as one of BRAVE CF\u2019s color commentators. Campbell was raised in the town of Larne in County Antrim and educated at Garron Tower College before earning an honours degree in Criminology and Criminal Justice from the University of Ulster. Prior to his broadcasting career, he competed in multiple combat sports, becoming an Irish National MMA Champion as well as an Irish National Bare-Knuckle Boxing Champion. His background as a fighter has contributed to his analytical style and technical breakdowns during BRAVE CF broadcasts.{{Cite web |date=2024-10-01 |title=Muhammad Mokaev's BRAVE CF return hailed by commentator: 'The sky really is the limit' {{!}} Sporting News |url=https://www.sportingnews.com/us/mma/news/muhammad-mokaev-brave-cf-return-hailed/7a05037da598c865d405e8fc |access-date=2025-11-15 |website=www.sportingnews.com |language=en-us}}\n\n==== Kirik Jenness ====\nKirik Jenness, a long-time figure within the global mixed martial arts community, joined BRAVE CF\u2019s broadcast team as an analyst.{{Cite news |last=Al-Shatti |first=Shaheen |date=2020-01-30 |title=High schoolers, liars and death threats: Welcome to the treacherous world of MMA record-keeping |url=https://www.nytimes.com/athletic/1571911/2020/01/30/high-schoolers-liars-and-death-threats-welcome-to-the-treacherous-world-of-mma-record-keeping/ |access-date=2025-11-15 |work=The New York Times |language=en-US |issn=0362-4331}} Jenness is known for his role as the official keeper of MMA records under the Muhammad Ali Boxing Reform Act in the United States, as well as for providing expert commentary for events in more than 30 countries.{{Cite web |last=Lee |first=Alexander K. |date=2020-07-05 |title=Behind the panel: A closer look at the UFC's rankings system |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/5/21308035/behind-the-panel-a-closer-look-at-the-ufcs-rankings-system |access-date=2025-11-15 |website=MMA Fighting: UFC, Mixed Martial Arts (MMA) News, Results |language=en-US}} In 2022, he received the World MMA Awards Lifetime Achievement Award, recognizing decades of contributions to the sport.{{Cite web |title=Fighters Only : The OG of the UG: Kirik Jenness on watching MMA explode into the mainstream |url=https://fightersonly.com/article/ext/82853/The+MMA+Biz/1 |access-date=2025-11-15 |website=fightersonly.com |language=en}}\n\n==== Carlos Kremer ====\nCarlos Kremer serves as BRAVE CF\u2019s official cage announcer{{Cite web |date=2017-12-05 |title=Other Sports: Cage announcer Kremer claims Fightbook title |url=https://www.gdnonline.com/Details/299949/Cage-announcer-Kremer-claims-Fightbook-title |access-date=2025-11-15 |website=www.gdnonline.com}} and has been part of the promotion since BRAVE CF 1. A former U.S. Marine Corps Captain and media personality,{{Cite web |title=Carlos Kremer {{!}} Actor |url=https://www.imdb.com/name/nm3215952/ |access-date=2025-11-19 |website=IMDb |language=en-US}} Kremer has become one of the longest-serving voices of the organization. In 2025, during BRAVE CF 100, he was inducted into the BRAVE CF Hall of Fame,{{Cite web |title=Bahrain News Agency |url=https://www.bna.bh/en/index.aspx |access-date=2025-11-15 |website=www.bna.bh}} becoming the first cage announcer to receive this distinction. Kremer graduated from UC Davis and played Collegiate Football before serving as a US Marine Corps Captain and recipient of Two Navy Achievement Medals for Honor and Valor. He was the only Marine out of 42 Marines to escape SERE School ( Survival, Evasion, Resistance and Escape), and was selected to lead Marine Boot Camp at MCRD.{{Cite web |date=2018-04-30 |title=Kremer returns to host Brave 12 {{!}} THE DAILY TRIBUNE {{!}} KINGDOM OF BAHRAIN |url=https://www.newsofbahrain.com/bahrain/44188.html |access-date=2025-11-19 |website=DT News |language=en-US}} He was also inducted to the Hall of Fame of the USA Martial Arts in the class of 2024. Kremer is married to Teca Kremer and has two children.{{Cite web |last=Clifton |first= Michael |date=2021-06-01 |title=Exclusive: How Carlos Kremer Became The Voice Of BRAVE CF |url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/exclusive-how-carlos-kremer-became-the-voice-of-brave-cf/ |access-date=2025-11-19 |website=www.lowkickmma.com |language=en-US}}\n\n===TV partners===\n* [[Bahrain Radio and Television Corporation]]\n* [[OSN Sports]]\n* [[El Rey Network]]\n* [[Abu Dhabi Sports]]{{cite web |date=19 November 2018 |title=Brave 18 breaks record for broadcasting reach |url=https://www.bahrainthisweek.com/brave-18-breaks-record-for-broadcasting-reach/ |access-date=May 15, 2019}}\n* [[Globosat|Combate]]{{cite web |title=UFC Fight Night, Abu Dhabi World Pro e Brave 5 agitam o Combate |url=http://www.lance.com.br/lutas/ufc-fight-night-abu-dhabi-world-pro-brave-agitam-combate.html |website=www.lance.com.br}}\n* [[Myx TV]]{{cite web |title=Myx TV - Brave Combat Federation |url=http://myxtv.com/shows/brave-combat-federation |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170224053659/http://myxtv.com/shows/brave-combat-federation |archive-date=2017-02-24 |access-date=2017-02-23}}\n* [[ABS-CBN Sports and Action|S+A]]\n* [[ESPN 5]]\n* [[MTV India]]{{cite news |last1=Laghate |first1=Gaurav |date=19 December 2018 |title=MTV, Mercury Sports sign multi-year deal to bring BRAVE fights, reality show on TV |url=https://economictimes.indiatimes.com/industry/media/entertainment/mtv-mercury-sports-sign-multi-year-deal-to-bring-brave-fights-reality-show-on-tv/articleshow/67157002.cms |access-date=December 19, 2018 |newspaper=The Economic Times}}\n* Fite TV{{cite web |title=BRAVE Combat Federation Streams & Videos |url=https://www.fite.tv/vl/p/brave-combat-federation/ |access-date=May 15, 2019}}\n* [[Polsat Sport]]\n\n==BRAVE TV & BRAVE Films==\n[[File:Poster_of_SMILE.jpg|thumb|right|Poster of SMILE]]\nBRAVE Combat Federation announced the initiation of operations of their subsidiary film production unit named BRAVE Films. To commence operations the film production unit produced the television documentary-drama \"SMILE\"{{Cite web|url=https://www.imdb.com/title/tt7074622/|title=Smile|date=June 26, 2017|via=IMDb}} on 26 June during Eid.{{Cite web|url=http://www.gdnonline.com/Details/220315/Brave-launch-SMILE-trailer|title=Other Sports: Brave launch SMILE trailer|website=www.gdnonline.com|date=5 December 2017 }} In 2024, BRAVE CF produced two documentaries, sharing the narratives of Hamza Kooheji (Bahrain) and [[Muhammad Mokaev]] (United Kingdom).\n\n'''BRAVE TV:''' BRAVE Combat Federation announced last year the creation of BRAVE TV, an online subscription service in which fans would be able to watch live events, as well as reruns of old shows. BRAVE 23 was available on the newly formed online streaming platform to fans from all over the world, except for those territories contemplated with preexisting TV deals.{{cite web |title=BRAVE COMBAT FEDERATION TO LIVE STREAM HISTORICAL JORDAN EVENT FOR FREE |url=https://www.bravefights.com/brave-combat-federation-to-live-stream-historical-jordan-event-for-free/ |publisher=BRAVE Fights}}\n\n'''SMILE:''' The short film narrates the story of the featherweight champion of BRAVE Combat Federation and behind the scenes of his training leading to the championship bout. The content was shot extensively in [[Montpellier]], France and Tijuana, [[Mexico]] apart from locations in [[Bahrain]] and in [[Abu Dhabi]], [[United Arab Emirates]]. The initiative was publicized to popularise mixed martial arts and to showcase the lifestyle of fighters{{Cite web|url=https://thefangarage.com/articles/11097-brave-launches-trailer-for-documentary-based-on-the-life-of-elias-boudegezdane|title=Brave Launches Trailer For Documentary Based On The Life Of Elias Boudegezdane | The Fan Garage (TFG)|date=June 14, 2017|website=thefangarage.com}} and marks the launch of \"BRAVE Films\", a film production segment by BRAVE Combat Federation.{{Cite web|url=https://www.arabsmma.com/trailer-mma-film-smile-boudegzdame/|title=Trailer of MMA based film 'SMILE' featuring Boudegzdame|author=Arabs MMA Staff|date=June 13, 2017|website=ArabsMMA}}\n\n'''The Untold Story Hamza Kooheji:''' The documentary premiered focusing on the career of Bahraini MMA fighter Hamza Kooheji.{{cite web |last1=Siddhique |first1=Muhammad |title=Hamza Kooheji's Story Hits the Big Screen in an Exclusive Premiere |url=https://www.newsofbahrain.com/sports/106115.html |website=The Daily Tribune |publisher=News of Bahrain |access-date=28 December 2024}}\n\n'''Beyond the UFC: An Inside Look at BRAVE CF 91:''' The documentary, directed by MMA filmmaker Will Harris{{cite web |last1=Villa |first1=Roberto |title=Legendary MMA Filmmaker Will Harris to Shoot BRAVE 91 Documentary in Bahrain |url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/legendary-mma-filmmaker-will-harris-documents-brave-91-in-bahrain/ |website=FightBook MMA |publisher=FightBook MMA |access-date=28 December 2024}} provides an inside look at Muhammad Mokaev's return to BRAVE CF at BRAVE CF 91 and offers a behind-the-scenes perspective on Mokaev's training at KHK MMA.{{cite web |last1=Carrano |first1=Lucas |title=Will Harris Unveils a Gripping New Documentary on Muhammad Mokaev's Journey at BRAVE CF 91 |url=https://www.blackbeltmag.com/will-harris-unveils-a-gripping-new-documentary-on-muhammad-mokaev-s-journey-at-brave-cf-91 |website=Black Belt |publisher=Black Belt |access-date=28 December 2024}}\n\n==BRAVE International Combat Week==\nIn February 2017, KHK MMA and [[IMMAF]] announced, a partnership which would see the Amateur World Championships move from [[Las Vegas]] to [[Bahrain]] for the first time.{{cite web|url=https://immaf.org/event/2017-immaf-world-championships/|title=2017 IMMAF World Championships|publisher=IMMAF}} [[IMMAF]] president [[Kerrith Brown]] announced the shift for the world championships to move to Bahrain.{{cite web|url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/mma/mma-news-bahrain-to-host-2017-immaf-world-championships-of-amateur-mma|title=MMA News: Bahrain to host 2017 IMMAF World Championships of Amateur MMA|date=7 February 2017 |publisher=Sports Keeda}} In addition to the world championships, BRAVE president Mohammed \"The Hawk\" Shahid announced that the promotion would host a show in the same week as the World Championships.{{cite web|url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?time_continue=67&v=ECgGumM4-Nk|title=KHK MMA CEO confirms IMMAF World Championship during International Combat Week|date=9 February 2017 |publisher=YouTube}} The first International Combat Week was held from November 12 to November 19, with BRAVE 9 taking place on November 17, featuring two title fights. Subsequently, [[Bahrain]] hosted the Amateur World Championships for 2018{{cite web |title=BRAVE International Combat Week will cement Bahrain's international position |url=https://www.bna.bh/en/BRAVEInternationalCombatWeekwillcementBahrainsinternationalposition.aspx?cms=q8FmFJgiscL2fwIzON1%2BDgJ1toQfYlLVqhxjrhg9TpA%3D |publisher=Bahrain News Agency}} and 2019.{{cite web|url=https://immaf.org/2018-2019-immaf-world-championships-announced-for-bahrain-2/|title=2018 & 2019 IMMAF WORLD CHAMPIONSHIPS ANNOUNCED FOR BAHRAIN|date=8 March 2018 |publisher=IMMAF}}{{cite web|url=https://www.arabsmma.com/brave-18-results-al-selwady-destroys-x-ufc-fighter-to-become-brave-world-champion/|title=BRAVE 18 Results: Al-Selwady destroys X-UFC fighter to become BRAVE world champion|date=19 November 2018 |publisher=Arabs MMA}}\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of current Brave CF fighters]]\n\n== Notes ==\n{{Notelist}}\n\n==References==\n{{reflist}}\n\n{{MMA organizations}}\n{{Mixed martial arts champions}}\n\n[[Category:Mixed martial arts promotions]]\n[[Category:Mixed martial arts television shows]]\n[[Category:Brave Combat Federation|*]]\n[[Category:Mixed martial arts in Asia]]\n[[Category:Martial arts in Bahrain]]"}}}]},"17654013":{"pageid":17654013,"ns":0,"title":"Cage Warriors","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","*":"{{Short description|Mixed martial arts promotion company}}\n{{Redirect|CWFC}}\n{{Use dmy dates|date=July 2025}}\n{{Use British English|date=July 2025}}\n{{Infobox sports league\n| title = Cage Warriors\n| current_season = 2026 in Cage Warriors\n| logo = Cage Warriors logo.png\n| sport = [[Mixed martial arts]] [[promoter (entertainment)|promotion]]|\n| owner = Graham Boylan
Ian Dean\n| founded = {{start date and age|df=yes|2001||}}\n| founder = Dougie Truman\n| headquarters = [[London]], England\n| website = http://www.cagewarriors.com\n}}\n'''Cage Warriors''' ('''CW''') is an Irish-owned [[mixed martial arts]] (MMA) [[promoter (entertainment)|promotion company]] based in [[London]], England. The promotion was established in 2001 and staged its first [[Mixed Martial Arts|MMA]] event in London in July, 2002.\n\nMany MMA stars have made their names under the Cage Warriors banner, including [[Conor McGregor]], [[Michael Bisping]], [[Paddy Pimblett]], [[Gegard Mousasi]], [[Tom Aspinall]], [[Ant\u00f4nio Silva (fighter)|Ant\u00f4nio Silva]], [[Ian Machado Garry]], [[Martin Kampmann]], [[Dan Hardy]], [[Joseph Duffy (fighter)|Joe Duffy]], [[Cathal Pendred]], [[Neil Seery]], and [[Dennis Siver]].{{cite web|url=http://www.the42.ie/the-70-former-cage-warriors-fighters-who-ended-up-in-the-ufc-2701827-Apr2016/ |title=The 70 former Cage Warriors fighters who ended up in the UFC |website=The42.ie |date=8 April 2016 |access-date=14 April 2016}} CW are one of the few top promotions to take their events abroad, travelling throughout countries in [[Europe]], [[North America]] and the [[Middle East]].{{cite web |last=Bower |first=Mick |url=http://www.sabotagetimes.com/football-sport/mmas-arab-spring-how-cage-warriors-are-taking-over-the-middle-east/ |title=MMA's Arab Spring: How Cage Warriors Are Taking Over The Middle East |publisher=Sabotage Times |date=25 May 2012 |access-date=10 February 2014 |archive-date=29 August 2012 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120829011239/http://www.sabotagetimes.com/football-sport/mmas-arab-spring-how-cage-warriors-are-taking-over-the-middle-east/ |url-status=usurped }}\n\nThe promotion is watched globally via a host of television partners such as [[Viasat (Nordic television service)|Viasat]], and is one of the featured promotions on the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] (UFC)'s digital streaming service, [[UFC Fight Pass]]. It is currently being presented by UFC & BT Sport analyst [[Dan Hardy]], with commentators Josh Palmer and Brad Wharton.{{cite tweet|number=912644505672744960|user=CageWarriors|title=.@MMABrad48 and @JoshPalmerMMA...|date=26 September 2017}}\n\n==History==\n'''Cage Warriors Fighting Championship (CWFC)''' was founded by Dougie Truman in 2001; the first event, titled 'Armageddon', took place on 27 July 2002, and saw notable fighters such as Jean Silva, [[Rosi Sexton]], Matt Ewin and Paul Jenkins claim victories.{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/events/CWFC-1-Armaggeddon-1078 |title=CWFC 1 - Armageddon |publisher=Sherdog.com |date=27 July 2002 |access-date=10 February 2014}}\n\nIrishman Graham Boylan assumed control of Cage Warriors in 2010 and expanded the promotion's reach to mainland Europe and Asia, staging shows in England, Ireland, Russia, Ukraine, Jordan, United Arab Emirates, Lebanon and Bahrain.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmaweekly.com/cage-warriors-events-to-be-streamed-live-on-ufc-fight-pass |title=Cage Warriors Events to be Streamed Live on UFC Fight Pass |website=MMAWeekly.com |date=17 April 2016 |access-date=4 May 2016}} The Cage Warriors head office is located in [[London]].{{cite web |url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2015/2/11/8017991/cage-warriors-ceo-graham-boylan-steps-down-mma-news |title=Cage Warriors CEO resigns, promotion's future uncertain |publisher=Bloodyelbow.com |date=11 February 2015 |access-date=1 July 2015 |archive-date=4 March 2016 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160304055039/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2015/2/11/8017991/cage-warriors-ceo-graham-boylan-steps-down-mma-news |url-status=dead }}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/6/29/8862797/graham-boylan-acquires-cage-warriors-brand-eyes-potential-rebrand-and |title=Graham Boylan acquires Cage Warriors brand, eyes potential rebrand and two shows in 2015 |date=29 June 2015 |publisher=Mmafighting.com |access-date=1 July 2015}}\n\nFollowing Cage Warriors 74 in November 2014 the promotion went into hiatus, with Boylan announcing his resignation as CEO in February 2015.\n\nHowever, in June 2015, Boylan announced on ''[[The MMA Hour]]'' he had acquired the promotion and would be bringing it back to prominence again.{{Cite web|url=http://cagewarriors.com/former-cage-warriors-ceo-graham-boylan-acquires-the-cwfc-brand/|title=Former Cage Warriors CEO Graham Boylan acquires the CWFC brand - Cage Warriors|website=cagewarriors.com|language=en-GB|access-date=5 October 2017|archive-date=5 October 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20171005201816/http://cagewarriors.com/former-cage-warriors-ceo-graham-boylan-acquires-the-cwfc-brand/|url-status=dead}}\n\nThe promotion has since secured a digital broadcasting deal with the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] to have its shows shown live across the world on [[UFC Fight Pass]],{{Cite news|url=http://cagewarriors.com/partners/broadcast-partners/|title=Broadcast Partners - Cage Warriors|work=Cage Warriors|access-date=5 October 2017|language=en-GB|archive-date=5 October 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20171005151930/http://cagewarriors.com/partners/broadcast-partners/|url-status=dead}} and has also brokered television broadcast deals with major broadcasters across Europe and Asia.\n\nCage Warriors 114 marked the first time a mixed martial arts event in the [[United Kingdom]] was officially approved by the government.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/09/cage-warriors-makes-history-with-first-government-recognized-mma-shows-in-u-k|title=Cage Warriors makes history with first government-recognized MMA shows in U.K.|author=Simon Head|date=10 September 2020|work=[[MMAjunkie.com]]}}\n\n==Broadcast deals==\nCage Warriors Events are watched globally via a host of television partners such as [[Viasat (Nordic television service)|Viasat]], and is one of the few featured promotions on [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]]\u2019s digital streaming service [[UFC Fight Pass]].{{Cite web|url=https://cagewarriors.com/partners/broadcast-partners/|title=Broadcast Partners|website=Cage Warriors|language=en-GB|access-date=4 March 2020|archive-date=11 February athletic commissions, using the [[Unified Rules of Mixed Martial Arts]].\n\nCage Warriors bouts are officiated by top European officials, including British referees Marc Goddard and Rich Mitchell, and judged by world-experienced officials including Ben Cartlidge, David Lethaby and Mark Collett.\n\n==List of events==\n===Scheduled events===\n{| id=\"Scheduled events\" class=\"sortable wikitable Maciej Kawulski\n| headquarters = [[Warsaw]], Poland\n| sport = [[Mixed martial arts]] [[promoter (entertainment)|promotion]]| \n| owner = Private\n| website = {{URL|https://www.kswmma.com/}}\n| country = [[Poland]]\n| founded = {{start date and age|df=yes|2004||}}\n}}\n\n'''Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki''' (English: ''Martial Arts Confrontation''), better known by its initials '''KSW''', is widely considered to be the premier [[mixed martial arts]] organization in [[Poland]]{{cite web|last=Leidecker |first=Tim |url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/articles/Euro-Light-Heavyweight-Showdown-Ends-in-Draw-14388 |title=Euro Light Heavyweight Showdown Ends in Draw |publisher=Sherdog.com |date=2008-09-14 |access-date=2015-04-10}} and one of the leading in Europe.\n\n==Awards==\n*'''Violent Money TV'''\n**VMTV European Promotion of the Year 2022{{Cite web|url=https://www.vmtv.co.uk/2023/01/12/2022-mma-awards-show-fan-editors-edition/|title=2022 MMA AWARDS SHOW - FANS & EDITORS EDITION|work=Violent Money TV|first=Cauley |last=Quilty |date=2023-01-06}}\n\n==History==\n[[File:Hala Urania during KSW 108.jpg|thumb|right|260px|The cage during XTB KSW 108]]\nMartin Lewandowski had been the manager of the [[Centrum LIM|Hotel Marriott]] in Warsaw when he met Maciej Kawulski in 2002. Kawulski was organizing one of the biggest sports expos in Poland at that time, and as both businessmen come from martial arts backgrounds, they quickly found a common subject. Eighteen months later the first KSW show was born.{{cite web|last=Leidecker |first=Tim |url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/articles/The-Polish-Pride-14371 |title=The Polish Pride |publisher=Sherdog.com |date=2008-09-12 |access-date=2015-04-10}}\n\nThe promotion is famed for its four and eight-man tournaments, which until KSW X - Dekalog took place over the period of one night,{{cite web|last=Leidecker |first=Tim |url=http://sherdog.com/news/articles/the-polish-pride-14371 |title=The Polish Pride |publisher=Sherdog.com |date=2008-09-12 |access-date=2015-04-10}} but since KSW XI they are split, with the tournament final taking place on the next event. KSW XV is the first event without a new tournament, only with extra fights and finals from previous tournaments.\n\nDifferent from other bigger promotions such as the [[UFC]], KSW adopted the strategy of having events that are less frequent but bigger, focusing on creating big shows with stacked cards and with great spectacle.{{cite web\n| url=https://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/com-eventos-maiores-e-apoio-da-tv-aberta-ksw-peita-ufc-e-domina-mma-na-polonia.ghtml\n| trans-title=\n|url-status=\n| title=Com eventos maiores e apoio da TV aberta, KSW \"peita\" UFC e domina MMA na Pol\u00f4nia\n| last=Albuquerque\n| first=Adriano\n| author-link=\n| series=\n| volume=\n| issue=\n| editor=\n| publisher=\n| agency=\n| location=\n| page=\n| pages=\n| date=17 Oct 2017\n| access-date=4 Jan 2021 \n | language=\n| doi=\n| id=\n| archive-url=\n| archive-date=\n| quote=\n| chapter=\n| issn=\n| isbn=\n| type=\n| editor-last=\n| editor-first=\n| website=Combate\n}} In 2017, [[2017 in Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#KSW 39: Colosseum|KSW 39: Colosseum]] beat the record for the largest live attendance to a MMA event in Europe, and second-largest in history with 57,776 fans, only after the [[Pride Shockwave]] in 2002 and ahead of [[UFC 193]].{{Cite web|last=Zidan|first=Karim|date=2017-05-27|title=KSW 39 stadium show reportedly breaks UFC 193 attendance record|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/5/27/15704306/ksw-39-stadium-show-reportedly-breaks-ufc-193-attendance-record|access-date=2022-01-09|website=Bloody Elbow|language=en}}{{Cite web|title=Total Attendance|url=https://www.tapology.com/search/mma-event-figures/total-attendance|access-date=2022-01-09|website=Tapology|language=en}}\n\nSeveral fighters who have been successful in KSW have signed on to more well known promotions such as the [[UFC]]. KSW has its own fight team, the '''KSW Team''', which consists of some of the promotion's elite fighters, such as [[Mamed Khalidov]], [[Jan B\u0142achowicz]], Krzysztof Kulak, \u0141ukasz Jurkowski or Antoni Chmielewski. KSW co-operates with many other promotions in Europe by exchanging fighters, for example WFC and The Cage. KSW runs the majority of its shows live on a variety of TV networks ([[Polsat Sport]], [[Polsat Sport Extra]]) throughout Europe. Since KSW XI, the promotion runs its shows live on nationally available at private [[free-to-air]] television network [[Polsat]].\n\n===Viaplay era===\n\nOn November 22, 2021, KSW announced a long-term partnership with streaming platform Viaplay. Contract includes increasing number of KSW events to 12 per year and exclusive rights to Viaplay to broadcast KSW events on its platform for the Polish, Nordic, Scandinavian, Dutch and English markets.{{Cite web|date=2021-11-22|title=Viaplay secures exclusive KSW rights in 10 countries|url=https://www.viaplaygroup.com/news/news-releases/viaplay-secures-exclusive-ksw-rights-10-countries|website=Viaplay|language=en}}\n\n=== Cooperation with XTB ===\nIn October 2022, it was announced that as part of the extension of cooperation with [[XTB S.A.|XTB]] - one of the largest Polish fintechs offering its own investment platform, a new agreement was concluded, in which XTB will become the title sponsor of the KSW gala in 2023. The agreement is concluded for the entire year 2023 and will be valid for ten out of twelve planned galas, of which six of these events will be called XTB KSW. In other cases, XTB will be a strategic sponsor, just like in 2022 - at galas in [[\u0141\u00f3d\u017a]], [[Toru\u0144]], [[Radom]] and [[Warsaw]].{{Cite web |title = Nokaut! XTB sponsorem tytularnym gal jednej z najwi\u0119kszych i najbardziej rozpoznawalnych organizacji mieszanych sztuk walki na \u015bwiecie {{!}} |date = 2022-10-07 |url = https://www.fxmag.pl/artykul/nokaut-xtb-sponsorem-tytularnym-gal-jednej-z-najwiekszych-i-najbardziej-rozpoznawalnych-organizacji-mieszanych-sztuk-walki-na-swiecie |language = pl}}\n\n===Hall of Fame===\n\nOn May 10, 2023, the KSW federation announced the introduction of the Hall of Fame, intended to recognize players every year, as well as other people who have made a unique contribution to the history of the organization.{{cite web |date=2023-05-10 |language=pl |title=KSW wprowadza Hall of Fame {{!}} |url=https://www.mmarocks.pl/ksw/ksw-wprowadza-hall-of-fame}} The first honored player inducted into the Hall of Fame was former KSW commentator and the first KSW tournament champion, \u0141ukasz \"Juras\" Jurkowski [33] . The official ceremony of Jurkowski's introduction to the Hall of Fame took place during the XTB KSW 83: Colosseum 2 gala.{{Cite web |date = 2023-06-04|title = \u0141ukasz \"Juras\" Jurkowski w KSW Hall of Fame! \u2013 zobacz ceremoni\u0119 wprowadzenia na XTB KSW Colosseum 2 {{!}}|url = https://www.mmarocks.pl/ksw/lukasz-juras-jurkowski-w-ksw-hall-of-fame-zobacz-ceremonie-wprowadzenia-na-xtb-ksw-colosseum-2 |website = MMA Rocks!|language = pl}}\n\n=== Special XTB KSW Epic gala ===\nOn February 24, 2024, a special XTB KSW Epic gala took place, focusing on organizing fights in formulas other than just MMA.{{Cite web |title = \"Lista jest naprawd\u0119 przekozacka\" - Szef KSW o karcie walk XTB KSW Epic {{!}}|date = 2023-12-19 |url = https://www.mmarocks.pl/ksw/lista-jest-naprawde-przekozacka-szef-ksw-o-karcie-walk-xtb-ksw-epic |language = pl-PL}} During the fight of the evening of this event, Mamed Chalidow and Tomasz Adamek crossed gloves in the boxing formula.{{Cite web |title = Tomasz Adamek b\u0119dzie walczy\u0142 z Mamedem Khalidovem! Wielka walka w KSW staje si\u0119 faktem. Jest oficjalne potwierdzenie |date = 2023-12-22 |url = https://sport.se.pl/sporty-walki/boks/tomasz-adamek-bedzie-walczyl-z-mamedem-khalidovem-wielka-walka-w-ksw-staje-sie-faktem-jest-oficjalne-potwierdzenie-aa-osv5-sHgM-DWWY.html |language = pl}}\n\nThe KSW [[ring announcer]] is Waldemar Kasta, a former rapper born in [[Wroc\u0142aw]].\n\n== Roster ==\n{{Main|List of current KSW fighters}}\n\n==Rankings==\n\nThe rankings for the KSW's fighters are It was created as a result of the Legacy Fighting Championship and Resurrection Fighting Alliance merger in 2016.\n\n==History==\n===Resurrection Fighting Alliance===\nOn October 11, 2012, officials for the Pompano Beach, Florida based MMA promotion '''[[Titan Fighting Championships]]''' (Titan FC) were acquired by the Resurrection Fighting Alliance (RFA), along with certain fighter contracts, as well as the organization's television deal with what is now AXS TV.{{cite web | url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Resurrection-Fighting-Alliance-Acquires-Titan-FC-Nets-Fighter-Contracts-AXS-TV-Deal-46453| title=Resurrection Fighting Alliance Acquires Titan FC, Nets Fighter Contracts, AXS TV Deal|publisher=Sherdog.com| access-date=September 16, 2014| last=Whitman| first=Mike}} The goal was to combine the talent of both organizations under one umbrella, the RFA. Titan FC owner and president Joe Kelly was brought in as the vice president of the newly merged company; and seasoned MMA manager and [[Black House (MMA)|Black House]] gym owner, [[Ed Soares]], acted as president.{{cite web | url=http://www.mmamania.com/2012/10/23/3541024/mma-titan-fighting-championship-rfa-ed-soares| title=Ed Soares: Titan Fighting Championship merger with Resurrection Fighting Alliance 'pretty much a done deal'| publisher=MMAmania.com| access-date=September 16, 2014| last=Haggerty| first=Kevin| date=23 October 2012}} Original Titan founder Joe Kelly bought back the organization (Titan FC) in 2013 and produced two more shows on his own: ''Titan FC 25: Lashley vs. Asplund'' in June, and ''Titan FC 26: Hallman vs. Hornbuckle'' in August, both of which were aired live on AXS TV.{{cite web | url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2014/1/8/5289178/titan-fighting-championship-begins-anew-with-event-headlined-by-ricci |title=Titan Fighting Championship begins anew with event headlined by Ricci vs. Gurgel on CBS Sports Network |date=8 January 2014 |publisher=MMA Fighting |access-date=2014-04-22}}\n\nOn November 2, 2012, RFA had its first show aired on AXS TV with ''Resurrection Fighting Alliance 4: Griffin vs. Escudero''.{{cite web | last=Staff |first=MMAjunkie |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2012/10/resurrection-fighting-alliance-acquires-titan-fighting-championship-axs-tv-deal |title=Resurrection Fighting Alliance acquires Titan Fighting Championship, AXS TV deal |date=13 October 2012 |publisher=MMAjunkie.com |access-date=2012-10-13}}\n\n====Events====\n{| class=\"sortable wikitable succession-box\" class=\"wikitable mw-collapsible mw-collapsed\" style=\"font-size:90%\"\n! scope=\"col\" | #\n! The promotion has held events in: Houston, Dallas, San Antonio, Allen,{{cite web | last=Welch |first=Matt |url=http://www.onlinestream247.com/ufclive/ |title=Legac FC MMA returns to Allen in February \u2014 Star Local: Sports |publisher=Starlocalmedia.com |date=2015-01-27 |access-date=2015-03-30}} Tulsa, and Duluth. Notable fighters that have fought for LFC include [[Carlos Diego Ferreira]], [[Leonard Garcia]], [[Jonathan Brookins]], [[Daniel Pineda (fighter)|Daniel Pineda]], [[Anthony Njokuani]], [[Paul Buentello]], [[James McSweeney]], [[Jay Hieron]], [[Evangelista Santos]], [[Ray Borg]], [[Tim Means]], [[Henry Briones]], [[Thomas Almeida]], [[Derrick Lewis]], [[Sage Northcutt]], [[Holly Holm]] and [[Valentina Shevchenko]].{{cite web | last=Wyman | first=Patrick |url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/articles/2/Sherdogs-Top-10-Regional-MMA-Promotions-84369 |title=Sherdog's Top 10: Regional MMA Promotions \u2014 No. 10 |publisher=Sherdog.com |access-date=2015-04-10}} Its live events and competitions have been broadcast on AXS TV in the United States and [[Esporte Interativo]] in Brazil.\n\nOn July 22, 2011, Legacy FC had its first show aired on HDNet with ''Legacy Fighting Championship 7: Dollar vs. Prater''. The organization agreed to a second extension to take them through 2016 with newly branded AXS TV.{{cite web | last=Calimbas |first=Mike Janeiro]] (6)\n*[[Caraguatatuba]] (2)\n*[[Recife]] (1)\n}}\n\n==Promotions==\nLegacy FC and Resurrection FA agreed to a co-promoted live event on May 8 that featured bouts with fighters from one organization battling the other. The event took place on May 8 and as part of the AXS TV FIGHTS franchise. The event was labeled [[AXS TV Fights|AXS TV FIGHTS: RFA VS. Legacy Superfight]]. The main event featured a title bout as the Legacy FC and RFA flyweight champions fought for the AXS TV Super Fight title. In addition two Legacy FC fighters competed to crown a new lightweight champion, and the RFA bantamweight champion defended his title against a top RFA contender.{{cite web | url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/pressreleases/AXS-TV-FIGHTS-to-Broadcast-Historic-CoPromotion-Between-RFA-and-Legacy-83735 |title=AXS TV FIGHTS to Broadcast Historic Co-Promotion Between RFA and Legacy |publisher=Sherdog.com |date=2015-03-24 |access-date=2015-04-10}}{{cite web | url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/3/21/8270323/rfa-and-legacy-fighting-championships-to-stage-co-promoted-event-of |title=RFA and Legacy Fighting Championships to stage co-promoted event of superfights |publisher=MMA Fighting |date=2015-03-21 |access-date=2015-04-10}}\n\n==Merger==\nIn September 2016 it was announced that Legacy FC & RFA would merge to create a new Legacy Fighting Alliance promotion.{{cite web | url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/09/legacy-fc-and-rfa-merge-to-create-new-legacy-fighting-alliance-promotion| title=Legacy FC and RFA merge to create new Legacy Fighting Alliance promotion| date=19 September 2016| publisher=mmajunkie.com}} LFA had its first event on January 13, 2017, with Legacy FC and RFA bantamweight titles unification as the main fight.{{cite web | url=https://bleacherreport.com/articles/2689240-legacy-fighting-alliance-wants-to-be-the-college-football-of-mma| title=Legacy Fighting Alliance Wants to Be the College Football of MMA| first=Patrick |last=Wyman| website=[[Bleacher Report]]| access-date=2 February 2017}}\n\n==Broadcasting==\nAfter about two and a half years of broadcasting Legacy Fighting Alliance, AXS TV was by owner Mark Cuban sold to Anthem Sports. Anthem disbanded the AXS TV Fights division. At [[UFC 244]] it was announced UFC Fight Pass secured an exclusive on LFA broadcasts.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2019/11/2/20945672/lfa-signs-broadcasting-deal-to-air-exclusively-on-ufc-fight-pass|title=LFA signs broadcasting deal to air exclusively on UFC Fight Pass|author=Alexander K. Lee|publisher=MMA Fighting|date=2 November 2019}} On November 8, 2021, it was announced that the broadcasting deal with the UFC was renewed to last through 2025.{{cite web|url=https://news.lfa.com/2021/11/08/ufc-fight-pass-and-lfa-extend-exclusive-multi-year-streaming-agreement/|title=UFC FIGHT PASS and LFA extend exclusive multi-year streaming agreement|date=November 8, 2021|work=Legacy Fighting Alliance}} On January 7, 2026, it was announced that the promotion has officially parted ways with UFC Fight Pass and signed a new broadcast deal with [[Vice TV]].{{Cite web |last=Heck |first=Mike |date=2026-01-07 |title=LFA gets new TV home after parting ways with UFC Fight Pass |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/latest-news/465082/lfa-gets-new-tv-home-after-parting-ways-with-ufc-fight-pass |access-date=2026-01-09 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en-US}}\n\n==Current Champions==\n{| class=\"wikitable\"\n! title = Oktagon MMA\n| current_season = 2026 in Oktagon MMA\n| logo = Oktagon logo.png\n| founder = Pavol Neruda (formerly \u0160ipkovsk\u00fd)
Ond\u0159ej Novotn\u00fd\n| headquarters = \n| sport = [[Mixed martial arts]] [[promoter (entertainment)|promotion]]|\n| owner = Private\n| website = {{URL|https://oktagonmma.com}}\n| country = [[Czech Republic]]
[[Slovakia]]\n| founded = {{start date and age|df=yes|2016|June|22}}\n}}\n\n'''Oktagon MMA''' is a [[mixed martial arts]] organization based in the [[Czech Republic]] and [[Slovakia]], considered the largest Czech-Slovak organization. The founders and owners are Pavol Neruda (formerly \u0160ipkovsk\u00fd) and Ond\u0159ej Novotn\u00fd.{{Cite web |last=B\u0142achut |first=Filip |date=2020-04-06 |title=Organizacja OKTAGON powo\u0142uje do \u017cycia turniej Underground [WIDEO] |url=https://inthecage.pl/organizacja-oktagon-powoluje-do-zycia-turniej-underground-wideo/ |access-date=2024-03-03 |website=InTheCage.pl |language=pl}}\n\n==History==\nThe organization was founded in June 2016, when it came up with the reality show ''Oktagon Challenge''. The project culminated in the ''Oktagon 1'' tournament. G\u00e1bor Bor\u00e1ros and Jakub B\u011ble faced each other in the final. The winner of the main match and the entire reality show was G\u00e1bor Bor\u00e1ros, and Oktagon started a series of gala evenings. The following 3 series brought several winners from Slovakia. Samuel Kri\u0161tofi\u010d, Karol Ry\u0161av\u00fd and [[Lucia Szabov\u00e1]] completed the collection of champions. The final of the 5th series took place at the ''Oktagon 20'' tournament, in which Roman Paulus won on points over Tom\u00e1\u0161 Zajac.{{Cite web |title=O organizaci |url=https://oktagonmma.cz/organizace-oktagon-mma/ |access-date=2024-03-03 |website=OKTAGON MMA |language=cs}}{{Cite web |title=Unik\u00e1tn\u00ed reality show! V Oktagonu sledujte cestu MMA bojovn\u00edk\u016f za titulem |url=https://isport.blesk.cz/clanek/ostatni-mma/282422/unikatni-reality-show-v-oktagonu-sledujte-cestu-mma-bojovniku-za-titulem.html |access-date=2024-03-03 |website=iSport.cz |language=cs}}\n\nOktagon organizes several tournaments every year in the largest Czech and Slovak arenas and cities as well as Germany and England. It created a reality show with professional fighters called ''Oktagon Challenge'', ''Project X'', which followed the journey of an ordinary person to the [[Mixed martial arts cage|cage]], ''Project Y'' - an all-female MMA reality show, and last but not least, a talk show about fighters and people from the ''Oktagon Majk'' scene. It also publishes the weekly ''Oktagon Magaz\u00edn'' which maps the scene of combat sports. The organization now has more than 450,000 fans on social networks and YouTube, where their videos had over 46 million views in 2024.\n\n== Roster ==\n{{Main|List of current Oktagon MMA fighters}}\n\n==Rankings==\n\nThe rankings for the Oktagon MMA's fighters are both recorded and updated when information has been obtained from the Oktagon's website.{{Cite web |title=Rankings |url=https://oktagonmma.com/rankings/ |access-date=2024-02-27 |website=OKTAGON MMA |language=en}}\n\n* ''Updated as of June 20, 2026.''\n\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|-\n! colspan=\"7\" style=\"background-color: #ccf; color: #000080; text-align: center;\" | '''Oktagon Rankings'''\n|-\n!style=\"width:1%;\"| Rank\n!style=\"width:17%;\"| Pound for Pound\n!style=\"width:17%;\"| Heavyweight\n!style=\"width:17%;\"| Light Heavyweight\n!style=\"width:17%;\"| Middleweight\n!style=\"width:17%;\"| Welterweight \n!style=\"width:17%;\"| Lightweight\n|-\n| align=center| C\n|{{n/a}}\n|{{flagicon|IRL}} [[Will Fleury]]\n|{{flagicon|IRL}} [[Will Fleury]]\n|{{flagicon|TUR}} Kerim Engizek
